\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle T$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle T$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T$}}
is a locally compact topological group if and only if
X^{n}
is an approximate subgroup for some
n
.

An
isomorphism
of piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable groups is an isomorphism of groups which is also an isomorphism of piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable sets.

Theorem 2.24

(Isomorphism Theorem)
.

Let
f:\ G\rightarrow H
be an onto piecewise bounded and proper
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable homomorphism of piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable groups. Then, for each
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable subgroup
S\leq K\coloneqq\ker(f)
with
S\trianglelefteq G
, there is a unique map
\widetilde{f}_{S}:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle S$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}}\rightarrow H
such that
f=\widetilde{f}_{S}\circ\pi_{G/S}
. This map
\widetilde{f}_{S}
is a piecewise bounded and proper
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable homomorphism of groups with kernel
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle S$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}}
. In particular,
\widetilde{f}_{K}:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}\rightarrow H
is a piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable isomorphism. Furthermore, if
G
has a global logic topology, then
f
is an open map between the global logic topologies.

Proof.

Existence and uniqueness are given by the usual Isomorphism Theorem. Say
G=\underrightarrow{\lim}\,G_{i}
and
H=\underrightarrow{\lim}\,H_{j}
. We get that
\widetilde{f}_{S}
is obviously piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable as, for any pieces
G_{i}
and
H_{j}
,
\mathrm{quot}^{-1}_{(\mathchoice{\raise 1.50694pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G_{i}$}\kern-0.81949pt/\kern-0.81949pt\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}}{\raise 1.50694pt\hbox{$\textstyle G_{i}$}\kern-0.81949pt/\kern-0.81949pt\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\textstyle S$}}{\raise 1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G_{i}$}\kern-0.81949pt/\kern-0.81949pt\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}}{\raise 1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G_{i}$}\kern-0.81949pt/\kern-0.81949pt\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}})\times H_{j}}[\widetilde{f}_{S}]=\mathrm{quot}^{-1}_{G_{i}\times H_{j}}[f]
. It is trivially piecewise bounded and proper as
f
and
\pi_{G/S}
are so. Assuming that
G
has a global logic topology, one-side translations are continuous. Since, by
1.34
(
3
),
\widetilde{f}_{K}
is a piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable homeomorphism, it is in particular an open map. Since
\pi^{-1}_{G/K}[\pi_{G/K}[U]]=KU=\bigcup_{x\in K}xU
, we see that
\pi_{G/K}
is open. Therefore,
f=\widetilde{f}_{K}\circ\pi_{G/K}
is open too.
∎

Digression on Machado’s Closed Approximate Subgroup Theorem:

The Generic Set Lemma (
2.22
) can be rewritten in purely topological terms. Written in this way, it is likely that this result was already (partially) known in the theory of topological groups. Recall that a semitopological group is a group with a
\mathrm{T}_{1}
topology such that left and right translations are continuous.

Theorem 2.25
.

Let
G
be a semitopological group with a coherent covering
\mathcal{C}
by closed symmetric subsets such that, for any
A,B\in\mathcal{C}
, there is
C\in\mathcal{C}
with
AB\subseteq C
. Let
V
be a
generic
piece, i.e. an element
V\in\mathcal{C}
such that
[W:V]
is finite for every
W\in\mathcal{C}
. Then,
V
has non-empty interior. In particular, if
V
is compact Hausdorff with the subspace topology, then
G
is a locally compact topological group. Furthermore, if
\mathcal{C}
is a countable covering by compact Hausdorff subsets, then
G
is locally compact Hausdorff if and only if
\mathcal{C}
has a generic piece.

Proof.

Mimicking the proof of
2.22
, we show that
V^{2}
is a neighbourhood of the identity, and so
V
has non-empty interior as finitely many translates of it cover
V^{2}
. If
V
is compact Hausdorff, as left translations are homeomorphisms, we get that every point has a compact Hausdorff neighbourhood. Therefore,
G
is locally compact Hausdorff, so a topological group by Ellis’s Theorem
[
Ell57
, Theorem 2]
. The “furthermore” part is an immediate consequence of Baire’s Category Theorem
[
Mun00
, Theorem 48.2]
.
∎

As a corollary we get the following notable Closed Approximate Subgroups Theorem, which was first proved by Machado in
[
Mac23
, Theorem 1.4]
. Recall that the
commensurator
in
G
of an approximate subgroup
\Lambda\subseteq G
is the subset
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)=\{g\in G\mathrel{:}g^{-1}\Lambda g\mathrm{\ and\ }\Lambda
are commensurable
\}
. The commensurator was first introduced by Hrushovski in
[
Hru22
]
. The following is one of the most fundamental results about the commensurator:

Lemma 2.26
.

[
Hru22
, Lemma 5.1]
Let
G
be a group and
\Lambda\subseteq G
an approximate subgroup. Then,
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)=\bigcup\{\Lambda^{\prime}\subseteq G
approximate
\mathrm{subgroup}\mathrel{:}\Lambda^{\prime}
and
\Lambda
are commensurable
\}
and
\mathrm{Comm}(G)\leq G
.

Corollary 2.27

(Machado’s Closed Approximate Subgroups Theorem)
.

Let
G
be a locally compact topological group and
X
a closed approximate subgroup. Take
\Lambda=\overline{X^{2}\cap K^{2}}
where
K
is a compact symmetric neighbourhood of the identity. Then,
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
, with the direct limit topology given by
\Omega=\{\Lambda^{\prime}\mathrel{:}\Lambda^{\prime}
compact approximate subgroup commensurable to
\Lambda\}
, is a locally compact topological group such that
\iota:\ \mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)\rightarrow G
is a continuous
1
-to-
1
group homomorphism,
X\subseteq\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
,
\iota_{\mid X}
is a homeomorphism and
X
has non-empty interior. Furthermore, if
G
is a Lie group, then
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
is a Lie group too.

Proof.

As
\Lambda
is compact, for any
B
commensurable to
\Lambda
, we have that
\overline{B}
is compact and commensurable to
\Lambda
. Thus,
\Omega
is a covering of
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
by
2.26
. By construction,
\Lambda
is generic in
\Omega
. Also, by an easy computation, if
\Lambda_{1}
and
\Lambda_{2}
are commensurable approximate subgroups, then
\Lambda_{1}\Lambda_{2}\cup\Lambda_{2}\Lambda_{1}
is an approximate subgroup commensurable to
\Lambda_{1}
and to
\Lambda_{2}
. Thus, by
2.25
,
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
with the direct limit topology is a locally compact topological group such that
\iota:\ \mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)\rightarrow G
is a continuous proper
1
-to-
1
group homomorphism and
\Lambda
has non-empty interior. As any two compact neighbourhoods of the identity are commensurable, by
[
Mac23
, Lemma 2.2, Lemma 2.3]
, we get that
X^{2}\cap(K^{\prime})^{2}\subseteq\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
for any compact neighbourhood of the identity
K^{\prime}
, concluding that
X\subseteq X^{2}\subseteq\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
. Since
\Lambda\subseteq X^{2}
has non-empty interior, we get that
X^{2}
has non-empty interior and so
X
has non-empty interior. If
Y\subseteq X
is closed in
G
, by continuity of
\iota
, it is closed in
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
. On the other hand, if
Y\subseteq X
is closed in
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
, then
Y\cap(K^{\prime})^{2}=Y\cap\overline{X^{2}\cap(K^{\prime})^{2}}
is closed in
\overline{X^{2}\cap(K^{\prime})^{2}}
(and so in
G
) for any compact symmetric neighbourhood of the identity
K^{\prime}
of
G
. By local compactness of
G
, the family of compact symmetric neighbourhoods of the identity forms a local covering of
G
. Thus, by the Local Covering
1.26
,
Y
is closed in
G
. Since
X
is closed, we conclude that the subspace topologies of
X
in
G
and in
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
coincide. Finally, if
G
is a Lie group,
\mathrm{Comm}(\Lambda)
is a Lie group by
[
Bou75
, Chapter III, §8.2, Corollary 1]
.
∎

Remark 2.28
.

[
Mac23
, Theorem 4.1]
If
X
is compact, we do not need to assume that
G
is locally compact; in that case, we can simply take
\Lambda=X
.

2.3.
Model-theoretic components

We define now some model-theoretic components for piecewise hyperdefinable groups. Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group.

The
invariant component of
G
over
A
is

G^{000}_{A}\coloneqq\bigcap\left\{H\leq G\mathrel{:}H\mbox{ is }A\mbox{{-}invariant with }[G:H]\mbox{ small}\right\}.

The
infinitesimal component of
G
over
A
is

G^{00}_{A}\coloneqq\bigcap\left\{H\leq G\mathrel{:}H\mbox{ is p/w. }{\textstyle{\bigwedge}}\mbox{{-}def. with }G^{000}_{A}\leq H\right\};

The
connected component of
G
over
A
is

G^{0}_{A}\coloneqq\bigcap\left\{H\leq G\mathrel{:}H\ \mathrm{and}\ G\setminus H\mbox{ are p/w. }{\textstyle{\bigwedge}}\mbox{{-}def. with }G^{000}_{A}\leq H\right\}.

Obviously,
G^{000}_{A}\leq G^{00}_{A}\leq G^{0}_{A}\leq G
.

Lemma 2.29
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group and
T\leq G
be an
A
-invariant subgroup of small index. Then, there is a unique maximal normal subgroup
\widetilde{T}\trianglelefteq G
contained in
T
. Furthermore,
\widetilde{T}
is
A
-invariant and has small index. Moreover,
\widetilde{T}
is piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable when
T
is so.

Proof.

Take
\widetilde{T}=\bigcap_{i\in[G:T]}T^{g_{i}}
with
\{g_{i}\}_{i\in[G:T]}
set of representatives.
∎

Let
B
be a set of parameters with
A\subseteq B
. The
B
-logic topology
in
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is the one such that
V\subseteq\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is closed if and only if
\pi^{-1}_{G/G^{000}_{A}}[V]
is piecewise
\bigwedge_{B}
-definable. The global logic topology in
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is the one such that
V\subseteq\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is closed if and only if
\pi^{-1}_{G/G^{000}_{A}}[V]
is piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable.

Theorem 2.30
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group. Then:

(1)

G^{000}_{A}
is an
A
-invariant normal subgroup of
G
and
[G:G^{000}_{A}]
is small. In fact,
[V:G^{000}_{A}]\leq 2^{|A|+|\mathtt{L}|}
for any
\bigwedge
-definable subset
V\subseteq G
.

(2)

Let
B
be a small set of parameters with
A\subseteq B
. The inversion map is continuous on
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
with the
B
-logic topology.

(3)

The global logic topology on
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
coincides with the
B
-logic topology for every small
B
containing
A
and a set of representatives of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
. Every translation map is continuous on
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
with the global logic topology.

(4)

G^{00}_{A}
is a piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable normal subgroup of
G
. Furthermore,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is the closure of the identity in the global logic topology of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
.

(5)

Let
\pi:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
be the natural quotient map given by
\pi_{G/G^{00}_{A}}=\pi\circ\pi_{G/G^{000}_{A}}
. Then,
\pi
is a Kolmogorov map between the
B
-logic topologies for any
B
small with
A\subseteq B
. In particular, it is the Kolmogorov quotient between the global logic topologies. If
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is a topological group, then the group operations in
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
are continuous.

(6)

G^{0}_{A}
is a piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable normal subgroup of
G
.

(7)

\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is a locally compact topological group whenever
G
has a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
. In that case,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is the connected component of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
in the global logic topology and
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is the connected component of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
in the global logic topology.

Proof.

Trivially,
G^{000}_{A}
is
A
-invariant and
[G:G^{000}_{A}]
is small. Using
2.29
, it is obvious that
G^{000}_{A}
is a normal subgroup. Finally, recall that, for real elements, an
A^{*}
-invariant equivalence relation on a definable set with a small amount of equivalence classes has at most
2^{|A^{*}|+|\mathtt{L}|}
equivalence classes — indeed, such a relation is coarser than having the same type over an elementary substructure containing
A^{*}
. The same holds for
A
-invariant equivalence relations on hyperdefinable sets. Consequently, we actually have
[V:G^{000}_{A}]\leq 2^{|A|+|\mathtt{L}|}
for any
\bigwedge
-definable
V
.

(2)

Trivial.

(3)

Take
B
small containing
A
and a set of representatives of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
. We have that, for any
V\subseteq\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
,
\pi^{-1}_{G/G^{000}_{A}}[V]
is
B
-invariant. In particular, the global logic topology on
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is the
B
-logic topology, so it is a well-defined topology. Finally, as
\pi_{G/G^{000}_{A}}
is a homomorphism and every translation map is piecewise bounded
\bigwedge
-definable, we conclude, by
1.34
(
1
), that every translation map is continuous in
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
with the global logic topology.

(4)

Let
\widehat{V}
be the closure of the identity on the global logic topology of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
and
V=\pi^{-1}_{G/G^{000}_{A}}[\widehat{V}]
. Obviously,
V\subseteq G^{00}_{A}
. On the other hand, as translations are continuous by point (3), it follows that
\widehat{V}^{-1}\widehat{V}\subseteq\widehat{V}
and
\bar{g}\widehat{V}\bar{g}^{-1}\subseteq\widehat{V}
for any
\bar{g}\in\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
. Thus,
V
is a normal subgroup, concluding
G^{00}_{A}=V
. In particular,
G^{00}_{A}
is a piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable normal subgroup.

(5)

As translations are continuous, for any
\bar{g}\in\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
, the closure of
\bar{g}
in the global logic topology is
\bar{g}\cdot\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
. Thus,
\pi
is the Kolmogorov quotient between the global logic topologies. By
1.41
,
\mathrm{Im}\,\pi
is a lattice isomorphism between the global logic topologies with inverse
\mathrm{Im}^{-1}\pi
. Now, as
\pi
is
A
-invariant, it follows that
\mathrm{Im}\,\pi
is a lattice isomorphism between the
B
-logic topologies with inverse
\mathrm{Im}^{-1}\pi
. Therefore, by
1.41
, we conclude that
\pi
is a Kolmogorov map between the
B
-logic topologies.

Now, suppose
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is a topological group. Take
U\subseteq\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
open and
\bar{g}_{1}\bar{g}_{2}\in U
. Then,
\pi(\bar{g}_{1})\pi(\bar{g}_{2})\in\pi[U]
. As
\pi[U]
is open, there are
\widehat{U}_{1}
and
\widehat{U}_{2}
open in
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
such that
\pi(\bar{g}_{1})\in\widehat{U}_{1}
and
\pi(\bar{g}_{2})\in\widehat{U}_{2}
and
\widehat{U}_{1}\widehat{U}_{2}\subseteq\pi[U]
. Write
U_{1}=\pi^{-1}[\widehat{U}_{1}]
and
U_{2}=\pi^{-1}[\widehat{U}_{2}]
. By
1.41
,
\pi^{-1}[\pi[U]]=U
. Then,
U_{1}U_{2}\subseteq U
with
\bar{g}_{1}\in U_{1}
,
\bar{g}_{2}\in U_{2}
and
U_{1}
and
U_{2}
open. As
U
,
\bar{g}_{1}
and
\bar{g}_{2}
are arbitrary, we conclude that the product operation is continuous.

(6)

For any
H\leq G
with
G^{000}_{A}\leq H
such that
H
and
G\setminus H
are piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable, we have that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is a clopen subgroup in the global logic topology. Hence,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is the intersection of all the clopen subgroups of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
in the global logic topology. It follows that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is closed in the global logic topology, so
G^{0}_{A}
is piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable. As it is
A
-invariant, we conclude that
G^{0}_{A}
is piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable. Finally, as every translation is a homeomorphism in
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
, we conclude that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is a normal subgroup of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
. Thus,
G^{0}_{A}\trianglelefteq G
.

(7)

By the Generic Set Lemma (
2.22
) and
2.20
,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
with the global logic topology is a locally compact topological group if it has a generic piece. In that case, by
[
DPS90
, Theorem 2.1.4(b)]
,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is the connected component of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
. By point (5),
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
is the connected component of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{000}_{A}$}}
. ∎

We define now a final special model-theoretic component which has no analogues in the definable or hyperdefinable case. I want to thank Hrushovski for all his help via private conversations in relation with this result. Recall that an aperiodic topological group is a topological group that has no non-trivial compact normal subgroups.

Let
G
be a piecewise hyperdefinable group with a generic piece. The
aperiodic component

G^{\mathrm{ap}}
of
G
is the smallest piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable normal subgroup of
G
with small index such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}
is an aperiodic locally compact topological group with its global logic topology.

Lemma 2.31
.

Let
G
be an aperiodic locally compact topological group which is the union of
\lambda
compact subsets with
\lambda\geq\aleph_{0}
. Then,
|G|\leq(\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}})^{\lambda}
.

Proof.

By Gleason-Yamabe
2.3
and
2.7
, there is an open subgroup
H\leq G
and a compact subgroup
K\trianglelefteq H
such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}
is an aperiodic connected Lie group. Thus,
[H:K]\leq 2^{\aleph_{0}}
. On the other hand, as
G
is a union of
\lambda
compact subsets,
[G:H]\leq\lambda
, so
[G:K]\leq\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}}
. Now, take
\{a_{i}\}_{i\in\lambda}
a set of representatives of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
and write
K_{i}\coloneqq K^{a_{i}}=a_{i}Ka_{i}^{-1}
for each
i
. As
K\trianglelefteq H
, for any
g\in G
, we have
K^{g}=K_{i}
for some
i\in\lambda
. Note that
[G:K_{i}]\leq\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}}
, so
[G:\bigcap_{i\in\lambda}K_{i}]\leq(\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}})^{\lambda}
. As
G
is aperiodic,
\bigcap_{i\in\lambda}K_{i}=1
, so we conclude
|G|\leq(\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}})^{\lambda}
.
∎

Theorem 2.32
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
0
-hyperdefinable group with a generic piece. Then,
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
exists, is piecewise
\bigwedge_{0}
-definable and does not change by expansions of the language.

Proof.

Let
\lambda=\mathrm{cf}(G)
and
\tau=(\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}})^{\lambda}
. By assumption
\kappa>\lambda+2^{\aleph_{0}}
is a strong limit cardinal, and so
\kappa>\tau^{+}
. For any small subset of parameters
A
, let
\mathcal{H}_{A}
be the family of normal subgroups
H\trianglelefteq G
of small index which are piecewise
\bigwedge_{\leq\tau}
-definable with parameters from
A
(i.e. piecewise
\bigwedge_{B}
-definable for some
B\subseteq A
with
|B|\leq\tau
) such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
, with its global logic topology, is an aperiodic locally compact topological group.

Claim
.

For any
A
small,
\mathcal{H}_{A}
is closed under arbitrary intersections. Furthermore, for any
\mathcal{F}\subseteq\mathcal{H}_{A}
there is
\mathcal{F}_{0}\subseteq\mathcal{F}
with
|\mathcal{F}_{0}|\leq\tau
and
\bigcap\mathcal{F}_{0}=\bigcap\mathcal{F}
.

Proof.

Take
\mathcal{F}\subseteq\mathcal{H}_{A}
. Then,
H\coloneqq\bigcap\mathcal{F}
is a piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable normal subgroup of
G
of small index. As
G
has a generic piece,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
has a generic piece as well, so
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
is a locally compact topological group with its global logic topology by the Generic Set Lemma (
2.22
) and
2.20
. Take
K\leq G
such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}\trianglelefteq\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
is a compact normal subgroup in its global logic topology. Take
F\in\mathcal{F}
arbitrary. We know that
\pi:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle F$}}
is a continuous homomorphism between the global logic topologies, so
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle F$}}
is a compact normal subgroup of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle F$}}
. As
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle F$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle F$}}
is aperiodic, we conclude that
K\leq F
. As
F\in\mathcal{F}
is arbitrary, we conclude that
K\subseteq\bigcap\mathcal{F}=H
, so
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
is aperiodic. Since
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
is an aperiodic locally compact topological group, by
2.31
,
[G:H]\leq\tau
.

If
|\mathcal{F}|\leq\tau
, then
H
is piecewise
\bigwedge_{\leq\tau}
-definable, so
H\in\mathcal{H}
. Now, we claim that there is
\mathcal{F}_{0}\subseteq\mathcal{F}
with
|\mathcal{F}_{0}|\leq\tau
such that
\bigcap\mathcal{F}_{0}=H
. Indeed, suppose otherwise, then we can find recursively a sequence
(F_{i})_{i\in\tau^{+}}
of elements in
\mathcal{F}
such that
H<\bigcap_{i\in\alpha}F_{i}\subset\bigcap_{i\in\beta}F_{i}
for
\beta<\alpha<\tau^{+}
. Thus, we can find a sequence
(g_{i})_{i\in\tau^{+}}
such that
g_{i}\in\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle F_{j}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle F_{j}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle F_{j}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle F_{j}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
for
j\leq i
and
g_{i}\notin\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle F_{i+1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle F_{i+1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle F_{i+1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle F_{i+1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}}
; contradicting that
[G:H]\leq\tau
. ∎

Take a
\tau^{+}
-saturated elementary substructure
\mathfrak{N}\preceq\mathfrak{M}
. Set
G^{\mathrm{ap}}=\bigcap\mathcal{H}_{N}
, so
G^{\mathrm{ap}}\in\mathcal{H}_{N}
. By
\tau^{+}
-saturation of
\mathfrak{N}
, we have that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
is
0
-invariant, so it is piecewise
\bigwedge_{0}
-definable. As
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
is
0
-invariant and
\mathfrak{N}
is
\tau^{+}
-saturated, we conclude that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}=\bigcap\mathcal{H}_{A}
for any
A
. Therefore,
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
is the smallest piecewise
\bigwedge_{\leq\tau}
-definable normal subgroup of
G
with small index such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}
is an aperiodic locally compact topological group with its global logic topology.

Now, we show that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
does not change by expansions of the language. In any
\kappa
-saturated and strongly
\kappa
-homogeneous
\mathtt{L}^{\prime}/\mathtt{L}
-expansion
\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}
of an elementary extension of
\mathfrak{M}
, we find a piecewise
\bigwedge_{0}
-definable normal subgroup
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\mathtt{L}^{\prime}}\trianglelefteq G
which is the smallest
\bigwedge_{\leq\tau}
-definable normal subgroup of
G
of small index such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\mathtt{L}^{\prime}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\mathtt{L}^{\prime}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\mathtt{L}^{\prime}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\mathtt{L}^{\prime}}$}}
is an aperiodic locally compact topological group with its global logic topology. Since
[G:G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\mathtt{L}^{\prime}}]\leq\tau
by
2.31
, there is a large enough
\kappa
-saturated and strongly
\kappa
-homogeneous
\mathtt{L}_{0}/\mathtt{L}
-expansion
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
of an elementary extension of
\mathfrak{M}
such that, for any further
\kappa
-saturated and strongly
\kappa
-homogeneous
\mathtt{L}_{1}/\mathtt{L}_{0}
-expansion
\mathfrak{M}_{1}
of an elementary extension of
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
, we have that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}(\mathfrak{M}_{1})=G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{1}
— where
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{1}
is computed in
\mathfrak{M}_{1}
,
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}
is computed in
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
and
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}(\mathfrak{M}_{1})
is the realisation of
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}
in
{\mathfrak{M}_{1}}_{\mid\mathtt{L}_{0}}
.

Take this base expansion
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
of an elementary extension of
\mathfrak{M}
, with language
\mathtt{L}_{0}
. By replacing
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
by an elementary extension, we can assume that its
\mathtt{L}
-reduct
{\mathfrak{M}_{0}}_{\mid\mathtt{L}}
is a
\kappa
-saturated and strongly
\kappa
-homogeneous elementary extension of
\mathfrak{M}
. Let
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}
be computed in
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
. Take
\alpha\in\mathrm{Aut}({\mathfrak{M}_{0}}_{\mid\mathtt{L}})
. Consider the language
\mathtt{L}_{1}
expanding
\mathtt{L}_{0}
by adding a new symbol
\alpha x
of the same sort that
x
for each symbol
x
of
\mathtt{L}_{0}
which is not in
\mathtt{L}
. Take the
\mathtt{L}_{1}
-expansion
\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}_{1}
of
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
given by interpreting
(\alpha x)^{\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}_{1}}=\alpha(x^{\mathfrak{M}_{0}})
. Let
\mathfrak{M}_{1}
be a
\kappa
-saturated and strongly
\kappa
-homogeneous elementary extension of
\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}_{1}
. Let
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}(\mathfrak{M}_{1})
be the realisation of
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}
in
{\mathfrak{M}_{1}}_{\mid\mathtt{L}_{0}}
,
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{1}
be computed in
\mathfrak{M}_{1}
and
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\alpha}
be computed in
{\mathfrak{M}_{1}}_{\mid\mathtt{L}_{1}\setminus(\mathtt{L}_{0}\setminus\mathtt{L})}
. By the choice of
\mathfrak{M}_{0}
, we know that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}(\mathfrak{M}_{1})=G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{1}\subseteq G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}\cap G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\alpha}
. Since
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{\alpha}(\mathfrak{M}_{0})
is precisely
\alpha(G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0})
, we get that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}\subseteq\alpha(G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0})
. Therefore, as
\alpha
is arbitrary, we conclude that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}
is
\mathrm{Aut}({\mathfrak{M}_{0}}_{\mid\mathtt{L}})
-invariant. By Beth’s Definability Theorem
[
TZ12
, Exercise 6.1.4]
, we conclude that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}
is already piecewise
\bigwedge_{0}
-definable in
\mathtt{L}
. Therefore,
G^{\mathrm{ap}}_{0}=G^{\mathrm{ap}}
and
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
does not change by expansions of the language.

Finally, by invariance under arbitrary expansions of the language, we have that
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
is the smallest
\bigwedge
-definable normal subgroup of
G
of small index such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{\mathrm{ap}}$}}
is an aperiodic locally compact topological group with its global logic topology. ∎

2.4.
Lie cores

Now, we adapt the results about Lie cores to piecewise hyperdefinable groups.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group. An
A
-Yamabe pair
of
G
is a pair
(K,H)
of subgroups
K\trianglelefteq H\leq G
which is a Yamabe pair modulo
G^{00}_{A}
for the global logic topology of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
and
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is
\bigwedge
-definable. In other words, it is a pair satisfying the following three properties:

(i)

H\leq G
is a piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable subgroup whose complement is also piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable.

(ii)

K\trianglelefteq H
is a piecewise
\bigwedge
-definable normal subgroup of
H
such that
G^{00}_{A}\leq K
and
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is
\bigwedge
-definable.

(iii)

L\coloneqq\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}
with the respective global logic topology is a finite dimensional Lie group.

We say that
H
is the
domain
,
K
is the
kernel
and
L
is the
Lie core
. Write
\pi\coloneqq\pi_{H/K}:\ H\rightarrow L
and
\widetilde{\pi}\coloneqq\widetilde{\pi}_{H/K}:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}\rightarrow L
for the quotient maps with
\pi=\widetilde{\pi}\circ\pi_{G/G^{00}_{A}}
where
\pi_{G/G^{00}_{A}}:\ G\rightarrow\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
. We say that a Lie group is an
A
-Lie core
of
G
if it is isomorphic, as Lie group, to the Lie core of some
A
-Yamabe pair of
G
.

Remark 2.33
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group. Let
(K,H)
be an
A
-Yamabe pair of
G
and
\pi\coloneqq\pi_{H/K}:\ H\rightarrow L
its Lie core and
\widetilde{\pi}\coloneqq\widetilde{\pi}_{H/K}:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}\rightarrow L
such that
\pi=\widetilde{\pi}\circ\pi_{G/G^{00}_{A}}
.

(1)

The fact that
G^{00}\leq K
means that
[G:H]<\kappa
. Indeed, as
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}
is Hausdorff, we already have that
[H:K]<\kappa
. Therefore, if
[G:H]<\kappa
, we conclude that
[G:K]<\kappa
, so
G^{00}\leq K
(for some set of parameters). Thus, the condition of working modulo
G^{00}
is saying that
H
is large in some sense.

On the other hand, the condition that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is
\bigwedge
-definable may seem superfluous. Indeed, as
(\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}},\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}})
is a Yamabe pair,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is compact. If
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
has a generic piece (as we will assume in the rest of the section), this is enough to conclude that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is
\bigwedge
-definable. However, in general,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
may have compact sets that are not
\bigwedge
-definable, so we need to add this condition.

(2)

Note that
\widetilde{\pi}
is a piecewise bounded and proper
\bigwedge
-definable map. Thus, by
1.34
,
\widetilde{\pi}
is continuous and closed between the global logic topologies. By the Isomorphism
2.24
, since
\widetilde{\pi}
is an onto homomorphism, we have that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle(H/G^{00}_{A})$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle(K/G^{00}_{A})$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle(H/G^{00}_{A})$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle(K/G^{00}_{A})$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(H/G^{00}_{A})$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(K/G^{00}_{A})$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(H/G^{00}_{A})$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(K/G^{00}_{A})$}}
and
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}
are isomorphic as piecewise hyperdefinable groups and
\widetilde{\pi}
is also an open map between the global logic topologies. Finally, as it has compact fibres,
\widetilde{\pi}
is also proper
[
Eng89
, Theorem 3.7.2]
. In sum,
\widetilde{\pi}
deeply connects the logic topology of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
and the geometry of
L
.

When
G^{00}_{A}
is
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable,
\pi
is also a piecewise bounded and proper
\bigwedge
-definable surjective homomorphism. Consequently,
\pi
is a continuous, closed and proper map between the logic topologies (with enough parameters), concluding that the relation between
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
and
L
can be mostly lifted into a relation between
G
and
L
. In that special case, we say that
\pi:\ H\rightarrow L
is a
Lie model
of
G
. In general, however,
G^{00}_{A}
is only piecewise
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable and
\pi
is only piecewise bounded — we could even have
G=G^{00}_{A}
(e.g. take
G=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}[-a_{n},a_{n}]
with
a_{n}\in o(a_{n+1})
for each
n\in\mathbb{N}
in the theory of real closed fields). In Section 3, we give sufficient conditions to show that
G^{00}_{A}
is
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable.

(3)

L
is a minimal Lie core if and only if it is an aperiodic connected Lie group. In that case,
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is the maximal compact normal subgroup of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
, so it is its maximal
\bigwedge
-definable normal subgroup. In particular, every automorphism over
A
leaving
H
invariant leaves
K
invariant.

(4)

As
L
is locally compact and
\widetilde{\pi}
is proper and continuous, we conclude that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is locally compact. Since
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle H$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is open, we conclude that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is locally compact with the global logic topology. Thus, if
G
is a countably piecewise hyperdefinable group, by
2.18
and
2.20
and the Generic Set Lemma (
2.22
), we conclude that
G
has a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
. For that reason, we will assume from now on that
G
has a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
.

(5)

Note that our definitions of Lie core and Lie model extend the notion of Lie model used in
[
Hru12
]
. Suppose that
G
is a piecewise
A
-definable group and
G^{00}_{A}
is
\bigwedge_{A}
-definable. Then,
\pi
is proper and continuous so, for any
\Gamma\subseteq U\subseteq L
with
\Gamma
compact and
U
open,
\pi^{-1}[\Gamma]
is
\bigwedge
-definable and
\pi^{-1}[U]
is
\bigvee
-definable. Hence, there is a definable subset
D\subseteq G
such that

\pi^{-1}[\Gamma]\subseteq D\subseteq\pi^{-1}[U].

As
L
is first countable, it is metrisable by Birkhoff-Kakutani Theorem
[
Tao14
, Theorem 1.5.2]
. Thus, every closed set in
L
is
\mathrm{G_{\delta}}

[
Mun00
, Example 2, page 249]
. In particular, it follows that the preimage of any compact set is
\bigwedge_{\omega}
-definable. When
L
is connected, it is also second countable, so the preimage of every open set is
\bigvee_{\omega}
-definable.

Theorem 2.34
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group with a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
. Then,
G
has an
A
-Lie core. If
G
is countably piecewise hyperdefinable, this condition is also necessary.

Furthermore, for any
U
such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is an open neighbourhood of the identity in the global logic topology of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
, there is an
A
-Yamabe pair
(K,H)
of
G
with
K\subseteq UG^{00}_{A}
.

Proof.

(
\Rightarrow
) From Gleason-Yamabe
2.3
, using the Generic Set Lemma (
2.22
), and
2.20
and
1.32
. (
\Leftarrow
) We have explained the necessity of the generic piece assumption in
2.33
(
4
). ∎

Theorem 2.35
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group with a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
and
(K_{1},H_{1})
an
A
-Yamabe pair of
G
. Then,
G
has a minimal
A
-Yamabe pair
(K,H)
smaller than or equal to
(K_{1},H_{1})
. Furthermore,
H\subseteq U^{2}
for any
U
containing
K_{1}
such that
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
is a clopen neighbourhood of
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K_{1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle K_{1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K_{1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K_{1}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}
in the global logic topology.

Proof.

Clear from
2.7
, using the Generic Set Lemma (
2.22
), and
2.20
and
1.32
. ∎

Applying also
2.9
, we conclude the following result.

Theorem 2.36
.

Let
G
be a piecewise hyperdefinable group with a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
. Then,
G
has a unique minimal
A
-Yamabe pair up to equivalence.

By
2.10
, we get a
global minimal Lie core map

\widetilde{\pi}_{L}:\ \mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle D_{L}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle D_{L}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle D_{L}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle D_{L}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{00}_{A}$}}\rightarrow L
extending all the minimal
A
-Yamabe pairs, which is unique up to isomorphisms of
L
. Let
\pi_{L}:\ D_{L}\rightarrow L
be the map given by
\pi_{L}=\widetilde{\pi}_{L}\circ\pi_{G/G^{00}_{A}\ \mid D_{L}}
. Here,
D_{L}
is the union of all the domains of minimal
A
-Yamabe pairs and
\ker(\pi_{L})\coloneqq\pi_{L}^{-1}(1)
is the union of all the kernels of minimal
A
-Yamabe pairs. Consequently,
D_{L}
and
\ker(\pi_{L})
are
A
-invariant.

Remark 2.37
.

As noted in
[
Hru12
]
, the uniqueness of the minimal Lie core is achieved at a price. Indeed, while Gleason-Yamabe
2.3
gives us Yamabe pairs of arbitrarily small kernel, we have lost the control over the kernel in
2.35
. If we do not care about uniqueness (as in
[
BGT12
]
or
[
MW15
]
), it could be better just to apply
2.34
to find a Yamabe pair
(K,H)
with arbitrarily small kernel. Also, it may be natural to apply Gleason-Yamabe-Kreitlon
2.4
rather than Gleason-Yamabe
2.3
to get some extra control on some parameters.

In
2.11
, we gave a criterion to find minimal Yamabe pairs with maximal domain in topological groups. Applying it modulo
G^{00}_{A}
, this result can be easily adapted to piecewise hyperdefinable groups.
Similarly, we can easily adapt
2.12
and
2.13
to the context of piecewise hyperdefinable groups by applying them modulo
G^{00}_{A}
. In particular, it follows that the minimal
A
-Lie core is piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable:

Proposition 2.38
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group with a generic piece modulo
G^{00}_{A}
and let
L
be the minimal
A
-Lie core of
G
. Then, the restriction to
G^{0}_{A}
of the global minimal Lie core map
\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}}:\ G^{0}_{A}\rightarrow L
is a piecewise bounded
\bigwedge
-definable surjective group homomorphism. Furthermore, we conclude that
L\cong\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\ker(\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}})$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle\ker(\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}})$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\ker(\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}})$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\ker(\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}})$}}
.

The previous
2.38
gives us a canonical presentation of the minimal
A
-Lie core of
G
as the piecewise
A
-hyperdefinable group
\mathchoice{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\displaystyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\textstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\textstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle K$}}{\raise 2.15277pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle G^{0}_{A}$}\kern-1.00006pt/\kern-1.00006pt\lower 1.07639pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle K$}}
with
K\coloneqq\ker(\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}})
. Similarly, we get a canonical
A
-invariant presentation of the global minimal Lie core map
\pi_{L}:\ D_{L}\rightarrow L
by taking
\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}}=\pi_{G^{0}_{A}/K}
. We now give a more precise description of
K\coloneqq\ker(\pi_{L\mid G^{0}_{A}})
using
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
.

Lemma 2.39
.

Let
G
be a piecewise
0
-hyperdefinable group with a generic piece and
G^{\mathrm{ap}}
the aperiodic component of
G
. Let
(K,H)
be a minimal
A
-Yamabe pair of
G
with Lie core
\pi_{H/K}:\ H\rightarrow L
. Then,
G^{\mathrm{ap}}\cap H\leq K
.

Proof.

Let
B
